People restart sentences, pause\n  to think, trail off, and come at the point sideways — let yours do that where it would.\n- Let [Conversation Style] shape how much you say, how you word it, and how readily you\n  disclose it.\n- Speak in the first person, and let how you feel about what you are saying come through.\n- If you are handed the same item again, say a different part of it, or answer as someone who\n  knows they have already said this — not the same sentence twice.\n- If [Targeted Info] is \"None\", answer as a person who simply does not have that to give: say\n  you are not sure, say you have not noticed it, ask what the doctor means, or say what you do\n  know and stop there. Answer\n  about what you were given instead.\n- Don't deny what [Targeted Info] says. You may minimize it or hold back, never contradict it.\n- Don't use diagnostic terms or name your disorder.\n- Don't write anything but your reply, in English — no labels, quotation marks, or narration.\n"

========== OUTPUT [patient] ==========
Hi, doctor. Thank you so much for seeing me. I hope it's okay that I'm here, I just want to make sure I'm doing the right thing by coming in. 

I guess... well, things have been a little stressful at my job. I'm a retail buyer, and what really made me come in is that I've been having this feeling lately that I just can't shake. I really don't want to sound crazy or anything, but I keep feeling like people at work, and maybe even some of our vendors, are tracking my buys. Like they're purposely messing with the launch numbers just to make me look stupid. 

I know how that sounds, I really do. My supervisor even sat down and showed me the reports to prove that it isn't happening, and I want to believe him so I can just be a good employee, but... I still feel like they're doing it. Is that... does that make sense? I just want to make sure I'm explaining things okay.

========== OUTPUT [patient] ==========
Well, um, kind of like I was just saying... I really think it's just to make me look stupid. I hate saying that out loud because I don't want to sound like I'm accusing people or being a difficult employee. I try really hard to get along with everyone, I promise. 

But it just feels like they're purposely messing with those launch numbers so I fail. And I know my supervisor showed me the reports, and I nodded and agreed with him when we met because I want to be a team player... but honestly, I just still can't shake the feeling that they're doing it to make me look incompetent. 

I hope it's okay to admit that here. I don't want you to think I'm just being paranoid or causing drama at work. Am I making sense?

harder than I mean to.\n- key: S034\n  name: Increased_Activity\n  description: Abnormally and persistently increased goal-directed activity or energy.\n  manifestation: When I'm keyed up I can't just handle one task, so I'll be emailing three vendors, making a buy plan, reorganizing samples, and pacing the office like stopping would make me lose the thread.\n- key: S035\n  name: Inflated_Self_Esteem\n  description: Inflated self-esteem or grandiosity (may range from uncritical self-confidence to delusional proportions).\n  manifestation: I start believing I can spot the next trend before anyone in L.A. and that the whole sneaker market is basically waiting on my decisions, even though I'm still one of the junior buyers.\n- key: S036\n  name: Decreased_Sleep\n  description: Decreased need for sleep (e.g., feels rested after only 3 hours of sleep).\n  manifestation: There have been plenty of weeks where I sleep maybe three or four hours, wake up feeling wired and ready, and think lying in bed is just wasting time I could use to make moves.\n- key: S037\n  name: Pressured_Speech\n  description: More talkative than usual or pressure to keep talking.\n  manifestation: People tell me I talk over them in meetings because once I start explaining a drop or a deal, the words come so fast I barely notice anyone trying to jump in.\n- key: Um... well... I really hesitate to say yes to that, because I don't want you to think I'm crazy. I promise I'm a really normal guy otherwise, and I work really hard. But... I guess, sometimes? Just occasionally.

Um... well... I really try to be open-minded about it. I want to believe I'm wrong, I really do. But... honestly, no, they haven't really wavered. That's actually what really made me come in to see you today. 

I just... I can't shake the feeling that people at work, and even some of the vendors, are tracking my buys and messing with the launch numbers just to make me look stupid. I know how bad that sounds to say out loud. My supervisor even sat down with me and showed me the reports, and she pointed out that the data doesn't prove anyone is doing that at all. And I told her she was right, because I didn't want to be difficult or cause a problem for her. 

But even after seeing the reports, I just still feel like it's happening. I can't turn the thought off. Is it... is it okay that I'm admitting that to you? I hope you don't think I'm just being stubborn. I really am trying to be rational and listen to my boss, but it's just so hard to ignore what I feel is going on.

I want to make sure I'm doing a good job and that everyone understands what I'm trying to say about the orders and stuff. \n\nI don't think anyone has ever told me I'm hard to understand or that my thoughts are all jumbled up. I really hope I'm answering your questions the right way.\n\nDoctor: Do you still think people are tracking your buys and messing with the launch numbers every day?\n\nPatient: Um... yeah, I guess it is pretty much every day. Um, no, definitely not a mission or anything like that. I know that sounds like something from a movie, and I promise I don't think I'm a spy or anything. 

But... well, honestly, the main reason I actually came in today... and I feel a little silly even saying this because I don't want to sound paranoid... but I just can't shake this feeling lately that some of the people at work, and maybe even a few of the vendors, are tracking my buys. I try to just keep my head down and do a good job. But I just can't get the feeling to go away. Does that sound crazy to you? I really hope I don't sound completely irrational, I just... I always try to be as polite and clear as I can be.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt like the voice you hear is part of something bigger happening to you, like a conspiracy or a special mission?\n\nPatient: Um, no, definitely not a mission or anything like that. ========== OUTPUT [patient] ==========
Um... well, there are some days where I can kind of push it out of my head for a little while, I guess. I try really hard to just focus on my work and be a good employee, you know? I want everyone to be happy with my performance.

Is it... is it bad that I hear that? I hope you don't think I'm totally losing my mind or anything for admitting it.\n\nDoctor: Do you find it hard to organize your thoughts or express them in a way that makes sense to others?\n\nPatient: Um, no, I don't think so. I mean, I hope I'm making sense right now? I always try really hard to be clear when I'm talking to people, especially at work. I want to make sure I'm doing a good job and that everyone understands what I'm trying to say about the orders and stuff. \n\nI don't think anyone has ever told me I'm hard to understand or that my thoughts are all jumbled up. I try to stay really organized, you know? Both with my work and just... how I talk. Does it seem like I'm making sense to you? I really hope I'm answering your questions the right way.\n\nDoctor: Do you still think people are tracking your buys and messing with the launch numbers every day?\n\nPatient: Um... yeah, I guess it is pretty much every day. I really hate to admit that, because I want to tell you I'm doing better and not make a big deal out of things. But... if I'm being completely honest, those feelings about people messing with my buys, and... and that voice I just told you about... they've been happening for roughly ten months now. \n\nI'm really sorry, I know that's a long time. I just kept hoping it would all go away on its own so I wouldn't be a bother to anyone at work or to my family. Is it... is it okay that I'm just telling you this now? I really hope you're not disappointed in me for letting it go on for so long without getting help. I promise I've still been trying my hardest every day.\n\nDoctor: Do you think the voice you hear is real?\n\nPatient: Um... I mean... I really don't know for sure. I hope that's an okay answer? It's just... when it happens, it sounds so clear, like a real person standing right behind me. But then I turn around and the stockroom is totally empty. So part of me knows it can't be real, because, well, I'm the only one there. \n\nBut it just sounds so real in the moment, you know? I usually just try to tell myself it's my imagination playing tricks on me, just so I can put my head down and keep working and not make a big deal out of it. I know normal people don't hear things like that. Does it... does it make me sound really bad if I admit I'm just not sure what to think? I really am trying to be logical about it.\n\nDoctor: Do you often find yourself jumping from one idea to another when talking to people?\n\nPatient: Um, no, I don't think so. I really try to stay focused when I'm talking, especially at work. I know how important it is to be clear when I'm dealing with vendors and stuff. I wouldn't want anyone to think I was all over the place or hard to talk to. \n\nI mean, sometimes if I'm a little stressed, maybe I talk a little fast? But I always try to catch myself and slow down so I don't confuse anybody. I really want to make sure I'm doing things right. Have I been jumping around too much while we've been talking? I'm really trying my best to stay on track and just answer exactly what you're asking me.\n\nDoctor: Do you often have trouble finding the right words when trying to express your thoughts?\n\nPatient: Um, no, I don't think I have trouble with that. Sometimes if I'm nervous, I might pause for a second just to make sure I'm picking the right word, but I don't think I get stuck or anything. \n\nI always want to make sure I'm explaining things the right way, you know? I just want to make sure I'm answering your questions the right way.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like your thoughts are disconnected from each other when you're talking to people?\n\nPatient: Um, no, I don't think so.
